Feasible projection via bisection:
def project_feasible(h,n):
 h=np.clip(h,0,1)
 target=n/2
 s=h.sum()
 if s>target:
   # reduce: find α s.t. sum(max(0,h-α)) = target if majority not all zero. binary α 0..1. But subtracting uniformly changes lower bound; if h all >? 
   # Alternatively scale exact: h *= target/s if target/s>=0; no lower clip needed. Use scale for speed and preserve shape. But if h has many zeros, scale reduces small too. okay.
   if s>0: h = h*(target/s)
   else: h[:] = target/n
 else:
   # increase: bisection α 0..1: sum(min(1,h+α))
   if sum<target: lo=0, hi=1; for 60: mid; val=np.sum(np.minimum(1,h+mid)); if val<target lo=mid else hi=mid
   h=np.minimum(1,h+lo)
 h=np.clip(h,0,1)
 # fix exact sum: adjust one entry
 diff=target-h.sum()
 if abs(diff)>1e-12: find index with room if diff>0, set min(1,h+diff); else index positive set max(0,h+diff). But due sum maybe not exact. Could use while over indices.
 return h.

Formula for c_vec(idx m) where k=m-(n-1). For k>=0:
c[m] = sum_{i=0}^{n-1-k} h_i (1 - h_{i+k}) = sum_{i=0}^{n-1-k} h_i - sum_i h_i h_{i+k}.
Gradient for weight w:
grad[i] += w * (1 - h_{i+k}) for i=0..n-1-k
grad[i+k] += w * (-h_i) for i=0..n-1-k

Need derivative residual for least_squares:
Residual components:
R1 = f_A(h) - t (m)
R2 = G(h)^T λ - η 1 (n), where G is n×m gradient matrix (columns ∇f_a).
R3 = sum h - target (1)
R4 = sum λ -1 (1)
Jacobian:
- dR1/dh = H_A (m×n) [row a: Hessian of f_a]
- dR1/dt = -1 (m×1)
- dR1/dλ =0, dR1/dη=0
- dR2/dh = sum_a λ_a * d(∇f_a)/dh = λ^T Hessians? It's n×n matrix: for each h_j, ∂(∑λ ∂f_a/∂h_j)/∂h_i = ∑λ H_{a,i,j}. So J_h = H^T? Let M = weighted sum of Hessians of f_a. Then R2_i = ∑_a λ_a (∇f_a)_i - η. dR2_i/dh_j = (M)_{i,j}.
- dR2/dλ = G (n×m)
- dR2/dη = -1 vector (n×1)
- dR3/dh=1, others 0.
- dR4/dλ=1.
This is large but feasible for m small.

Hessian of f_k: f_k = ∑_{i=0}^{n-1-k} h_i - ∑ h_i h_{i+k} for k≥0; for k<0 similar. Hessian has -2 at pairs (i,i+k) and (i+k,i). So H_a is sparse banded. We can build dense H (m×n) rows and its transpose? Gradient G (n×m) known. To compute R2 = G^T λ - η*1 (where each column ∇). To compute Jacobian w.r.t h M = ∑ λ_a H_a. H_a dense but banded. We can fill M by loops over active: for each pair (i,j) with product term, add -2λ to M[i,j], M[j,i]. No linear terms Hessian. For negative shifts similar. O(m n). M symmetric.

Let's implement KKT Newton with active set dynamic:
Function `kkt_refine(h, n, deadline, max_iter=20, tol=1e-10)`:
- h = project_feasible.
- best c.
- active initially top indices where c >= c.max()-tol_active (tol_active=1e-4? maybe 1e-5). m maybe min(8, len).
- Initialize λ: weights of LSE with alpha=2000 normalized over active, or uniform. η = average gradient? t=c.max().
- Loop:
   Compute c, active maybe update if new constraints above t+1e-8? At KKT, active constraints should be <=t but equal for active. If some inactive >t, add to active (top). If λ for active < -1e-6, drop? We'll manage.
   Solve Newton for h,t,λ,η.
   After update, project h to feasible; clip t≥0; λ = max(λ,0)? Drop if too negative.
   Evaluate c; if c<best improve.
- Use damping: try full if decrease in c or residual norm; else halve step.

But constructing Jacobian M for h every iteration. Let's derive active gradients and Hessians.

Representation of shift index `idx` from 0 to 2n-2, k = idx-(n-1).

Implementation:
```
def c_vec_fft(h,n):
    L = 1 << (2*n-1).bit_length()
    Hv = np.fft.rfft(h, L)
    vf = np.fft.rfft(1-np.flip(h), L)
    conv = np.fft.irfft(Hv * vf, L)[:2*n-1]
    return conv[::-1] * (2.0/n)
```
Wait v=1-h not flip. conv(flip(h), v). Yes. For n=2 flip h=[b,a], v=[1-a,1-b]; conv=[b(1-a), b(1-b)+a(1-a), a(1-b)]. Reversed = [a(1-b), a(1-a)+b(1-b), b(1-a)]. Matches np.correlate. Good.
FFT approximate errors O(1e-15). Use for objective/gradient during optimization. For exact final, use correlate.

Implement top-level functions:
```
def lse_fg(x,n,alpha,mu):
   c=c_vec_fft(x,n)
   m=c.max()
   z=alpha*(c-m)
   zmax=z.max()
   e=np.exp(z-zmax)
   s=e.sum()
   val=m+(zmax+np.log(s))/alpha + mu*(x.sum()-n/2)**2
   w=e/s
   g=grad_from_weights(x,w,n)
   g += 2*mu*(x.sum()-n/2)
   return val,g
```

Detailed LFSR:
```
def lfsr_bits(deg=10, taps=[9,5], period=None):
    # generate maximal sequence length 2^deg-1, values 0/1
    state = 1 << (deg-1) maybe
    seq=[]
    L=1<<deg
    for i in range(period):
       seq.append(state & 1)
       feedback = (state >> taps[0] ^ state >> taps[1]) &1? Need correct taps.
       state = (state >>1) | (feedback<<(deg-1))
```

Feasibility project exact:
```
def project_feasible(h,n):
    h=np.asarray(h,float).copy()
    h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    target=n*0.5
    s=h.sum()
    if s < target:
       lo=0; hi=1
       for _ in range(60):
          mid=(lo+hi)/2
          val=np.sum(np.minimum(1.0, h+mid))
          if val < target: lo=mid
          else: hi=mid
       h=np.minimum(1.0,h+lo)
    elif s > target:
       # use uniform subtraction? To preserve relative differences? Use bisection α: max(0,h-α)
       lo=0; hi=1
       for _ in range(60):
          mid=(lo+hi)/2
          val=np.sum(np.maximum(0.0, h-mid))
          if val > target: lo=mid
          else: hi=mid
       h=np.maximum(0.0,h-lo)
    # exact adjust
    diff=target-h.sum()
    for _ in range(10):
       if abs(diff)<1e-13: break
       if diff>0:
          idx=int(np.argmin(h)) # most room? but if h=1? choose min
          if h[idx] < 1:
             add=min(diff,1-h[idx])
             h[idx]+=add
             diff-=add
       else:
          idx=int(np.argmax(h))
          if h[idx] >0:
             sub=min(-diff,h[idx])
             h[idx]-=sub
             diff+=sub
    return np.clip(h,0,1)
```
Uniform add/sub may change shape more than proportional. For subtract, if s>target, scale h*=target/s is exact and less disturbance (if no zeros? Actually scaling all, lower values reduce; but feasible). Use scale for subtract to avoid hitting 0. For adding, could scale room? Use uniform add bisection. But if h has values near1, uniform add only increases lower values until they catch; that's L_infinity projection onto sum hyperplane? It may be okay. More shape-preserving for add: solve h += α*(1-h) (?). Let's use previous proportional method with few iterations (fast):
```
for _ in range(100):
 diff=target-h.sum()
 if abs(diff)<1e-14 break
 if diff>0:
   room=1-h
   tot=room.sum()
   if tot<1e-14 break
   step=min(diff/tot, 1?) ; h += step*room
 else:
   tot=h.sum()
   if tot<1e-14 break
   h += (diff/tot)*h
```
This preserves shape and converges in few. Use 20 iterations enough. Then exact adjust. This is fast. Use this.

Constructing gradient for each active shift for LP:
```
def shift_grad(h,n,idx):
   dx=2/n; g=np.zeros(n); k=idx-(n-1)
   if k>=0: nv=n-k; g[:nv] += (1-h[k:])*dx; g[k:] += -h[:nv]*dx
   else: kk=-k; nv=n-kk; g[kk:] += (1-h[:nv])*dx; g[:nv] += -h[kk:]*dx
   return g
```

Delta bounds individual. This reduces LP size to active + variables n+1. Bounds on delta. That's much faster.

def line_search_delta(h,delta,n,deadline,exact_check=True):
   best_c=exact_c(h,n)
   best_alpha=0
   for alpha in [1,0.7,0.5,0.3,0.1,0.05,0.025]:
      if time.time()>deadline: break
      h_new=h+alpha*delta
      # due float, project slight: if any clip? delta bounds so within; maybe clip
      if h_new.min()<0 or max>1: h_new=np.clip; exact_c
      c=exact_c(h_new,n)
      if c<best_c: update alpha
   if best_alpha>0: return h+alpha*delta, c
   return h,best_c
```

def random_fourier_h(n, seed, modes=12):
  rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
  x=np.linspace(0,2,n)
  y=np.zeros(n)
  for m in range(1,modes+1):
    amp=1.0/(m**1.5)
    phi=rng.uniform(0,2*pi)
    y += amp*np.cos(0.5*pi*m*x+phi)  # with endpoint? Use cos(m*pi*x/2)
  # also sin? Need zero mean at endpoints? Not.
  y -= y.mean()
  scale = 1.0 / (np.max(np.abs(y))+1e-12)
  y *= 0.9*scale? want clip.
  h=0.5+y
  h=np.clip(h,0,1)
  return project_feasible(h,n)
```

```
import numpy as np, time
from scipy.optimize import minimize, linprog
import math

_DEADLINE = [0.0]

def time_callback(x):
    return time.time() > _DEADLINE[0]

def next_pow2(m): return 1 << (m-1).bit_length() maybe ensure >=m.

def c_vec_exact(h,n): return np.correlate(h, 1-h, 'full')*(2.0/n)
def c5_exact(h,n): return float(np.max(c_vec_exact(h,n)))

def c_vec_fft(h,n):
   L=...; hf=np.fft.rfft(h,L); vf=np.fft.rfft(1.0 - np.flip(h),L); conv=np.fft.irfft(hf*vf,L)[:2*n-1]; return conv[::-1]*(2.0/n)
```
Check FFT with `rfft` of real returns complex; product. `irfft` length L. Because convolution length 2n-1, zero padding good.
Need `np.flip(h)` returns new? okay.

Gradient from weights:
```
def grad_from_weights(x,w,n):
    g=np.zeros(n)
    dx=2.0/n
    g += w[n-1]*(1.0-2.0*x)
    # For d 1..n-1
    # We can loop; using local variables for speed
    for d in range(1,n):
        wp=w[n-1+d]; wn=w[n-1-d]
        s=wp+wn
        if s == 0.0: continue
        diff=wp-wn
        if diff != 0.0:
            base=diff
        else: base=0.0
        m=n-d
        # g[0:m] += diff - s*x[d:n]
        # g[d:n] += -diff - s*x[0:m]
        if s !=0:
           g[:m] += diff - s*x[d:]
           g[d:] += -diff - s*x[:m]
        else:
           # s zero but diff cannot if both zero; skip
           pass
    return g*dx
```
If weights underflow many zeros; still loop all n. Could precompute active weights indices where w>1e-15. But loop all n okay for n=1024; for 2048, 2047 loops. In each, `g[:m] += ...` allocates? It creates temporary length m; total O(n^2) memory allocations. Could be slow. Alternative loop over d but use `g[:m] += diff; g[:m] -= s*x[d:]`; two operations. Still allocations for x slab? Numpy views. Temporary for `diff - s*x[d:]` allocates. Could do two ufuncs:
```
g[:m] += diff

LSE fg:
```
def lse_fg(x,n,alpha,mu):
    c=c_vec_fft(x,n)
    m=c.max()
    z=alpha*(c-m)
    zm=z.max()
    e=np.exp(z-zm)
    s=e.sum()
    val = m + (zm + np.log(s))/alpha
    if mu !=0: val += mu*(x.sum()-0.5*n)**2
    w=e/s
    g=grad_from_weights(x,w,n)
    if mu !=0: g += 2*mu*(x.sum()-0.5*n)
    return float(val), g
```
If alpha very high, `zm` maybe 0 because c-m ≤0; e[max]=1, others underflow. w okay.
`c` could have tiny imaginary? irfft real.

Resample:
```
def resample(h,n):
   n_old=len(h)
   if n_old==n: return project_feasible(h,n)
   x_old=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n_old)
   x_new=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n)
   return project_feasible(np.interp(x_new,x_old,h),n)
```

LP functions:
```
def active_indices(cvec, max_cnt=40, tol_frac=0.003):
   cm=cvec.max(); tol=max(1e-9, tol_frac*cm)
   idx=np.where(cvec >= cm - tol)[0]
   if idx.size==0: idx=np.array([int(np.argmax(cvec))])
   elif idx.size>max_cnt:
      # keep top max_cnt by c, ensure max included
      order=np.argsort(-cvec[idx])[:max_cnt]
      idx=idx[order]
   return idx
```
LP:
```
def lp_step(h,n,active,rho):
   nv=n+1; c_obj=np.zeros(nv); c_obj[-1]=1.0
   cvec=c_vec_fft(h,n)
   A=np.zeros((len(active),nv)); b=np.zeros(len(active))
   for j,idx in enumerate(active):
       g=shift_grad_single(h,n,idx)
       A[j,:n]=g; A[j,n]=-1.0; b[j]=-cvec[idx]
   Aeq=np.ones((1,nv)); # but sum delta=0, t coefficient 0
   Aeq[0,n]=0.0
   bounds=[]
   for val in h:
       lo=-min(rho,val); hi=min(rho,1.0-val)
       bounds.append((lo,hi))
   bounds.append((0.0,1.0))
   try:
      res=linprog(c_obj,A_ub=A,b_ub=b,A_eq=Aeq,b_eq=np.zeros(1),bounds=bounds,method='highs')
      if res.success:
          return res.x[:n], res.x[n]
   except Exception:
      pass
   return None,None
```
`linprog` bounds list length n+1; for n=2048 constructing each call maybe okay.
Could use arrays lo/hi and bounds? SciPy linprog expects sequence tuples; array of 2D? It accepts list of (min,max). Constructing 2048 tuples per iteration maybe overhead. We can use `bounds=np.column_stack([lo,hi])`? SciPy accepts array_like shape (N,2) I think. Use list maybe okay. To save, use `bounds = [ (lo_i, hi_i) for ... ]`; 2048*100=204k tuples okay. Or use arrays: `bounds = np.asarray([lo,hi]).T`? Let's verify scipy linprog accepts bounds as array of shape (n,2)? It can accept list of tuples; I think array works. To be safe, use list.
A_eq only one row; b_eq [0].
Could set `options={'presolve': True}`? highspy default. Use no.

LP line search:
```
def try_delta(h, delta, n, deadline, alphas=(1,0.6,0.4,0.2,0.05)):
    best_h=h; best_c=c5_exact(h,n)
    for a in alphas:
       if time.time()>deadline: break
       h_new=h+a*delta
       # clip tiny
       if h_new.min() < -1e-12 or h_new.max() > 1+1e-12:
           h_new=np.clip(h_new,0,1)
       c=c5_exact(h_new,n)
       if c < best_c - 1e-14:
           best_h=h_new; best_c=c
    return best_h,best_c
```
Since delta bounds individual, α up to1 feasible; if float clip, sum may drift; after accept project? If we clip, sum changes. Use if any out due float, project_feasible. Better after h_new compute `if min < -1e-12 or max>... or abs(sum-target)>1e-10: h_new=project_feasible(h_new,n)`. Then evaluate.
For accepted h, project once and re-evaluate? `best_h=project_feasible(best_h,n); best_c=c5_exact(best_h,n)`. Good.

SQP loop:
```
def lp_polish(h,n,deadline,rho=0.05,max_iter=300):
   h=project_feasible(h,n); best_c=c5_exact(h,n)
   no_improve=0
   for it in range(max_iter):
      if time.time()>deadline: break
      cvec=c_vec_fft(h,n)
      active=active_indices(cvec, max_cnt=40, tol_frac=0.004)
      delta,t=lp_step(h,n,active,rho)
      if delta is None or np.max(np.abs(delta))<1e-13:
          rho *=0.7
          if rho<1e-6: break
          continue
      h_new,c_new=try_delta(h,delta,n,deadline)
      if c_new < best_c - 1e-13:
          h=h_new; best_c=c_new; no_improve=0
          if rho <0.1: rho=min(rho*1.15,0.12)
      else:
          no_improve+=1
          rho *=0.7
          if no_improve>15 or rho<1e-6: break
   return h,best_c
```
Maybe try multiple active tol fractions for no improve? We can loop over tol [0.001,0.003,0.01]. But time.

```
 if no_improve>5:
    val,grad=lse_fg(h,n,2000,0); d=-grad; d-=d.mean(); scale=rho/norm; line search; update if.
```
Implement in `lp_polish` occasionally (every 5 no improve) to avoid stuck. Need avoid too many exact evals.

L-BFGS function:
```
def run_lbfgs(h,n,deadline,alphas=(500,2000,8000,20000),mu=0.01,maxiter=300):
   h=project_feasible(h,n); best_c=c5_exact(h,n); best_h=h.copy()
   bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
   for alpha in alphas:
      if time.time()>deadline-5: break
      try:
        res=minimize(lse_fg, h, args=(n,alpha,mu), jac=True, method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds, callback=time_callback, options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-16,'gtol':1e-12,'maxfun':2000,'maxls':30})
      except Exception: return best_h,best_c
      h_new=project_feasible(res.x,n); c_new=c5_exact(h_new,n)
      if c_new < best_c - 1e-14:
          best_h=h_new; best_c=c_new; h=h_new
      elif c_new <= best_c + 1e-12:
          h=h_new
      else:
          h=best_h.copy()
   return best_h,best_c
```

def make_sum_exact(h,n):
 target=0.5*n; diff=target-h.sum()
 if abs(diff)>1e-12:
   if diff>0:
      order=np.argsort(h)
      rem=diff
      for i in order:
         room=1-h[i]
         if room>0:
            inc=min(rem,room)
            h[i]+=inc; rem-=inc
            if rem<=1e-14: break
   else:
      order=np.argsort(-h)
      rem=-diff
      for i in order:
         if h[i]>0:
            dec=min(rem,h[i])
            h[i]-=dec; rem-=dec
            if rem<=1e-14: break
 return h

def gradient_step(h,n,deadline,rho,alpha=2000):
   val,grad=lse_fg(h,n,alpha,0.0)
   d=-grad
   d -= d.mean()

   # clip to stay in bounds: find max alpha of d

def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
   t0=time.time()
   deadline=t0+max(10.0,float(budget_s))-5
   _DEADLINE[0]=deadline
   np.random.seed(seed)
   # get initial
   init_h=None
   try:
      init_h=np.array(initial_h_values,dtype=float).copy()
   except: init_h=None
   # initial best
   best_h,best_n=None,0; best_c=1e9
   if init_h is not None and len>1:
       n0=len(init_h); h0=project_feasible(init_h,n0); c0=c5_exact(h0,n0); best...
   else:
       h0=random_smooth_candidate(512,seed); ...
   # low-res global
   remaining=deadline-time.time()
   if remaining>180:
      n_low=256
      low_candidates=[]

      # allocate per cand

          hc,c=run_lbfgs(hc,n_low,t_phase, alphas=(300,1500,5000), mu=0.1, maxiter=120)
          if c<best_c: update
   # high main
   n_main = 1024 if remaining>700 else 512
   candidates=[]

   if best_h from low maybe: candidates.append(resample(best_h,n_main))

   t_now=time.time(); rem=deadline-t_now
   # allocate main high maybe 45% remaining

```

Implement `random_smooth_candidate(n, seed, kind)`:
```
def random_smooth_candidate(n, seed, kind=0):
    rng=np.random.RandomState((seed % (2**31-1)))
    x=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n)
    h=np.full(n,0.5)
    if kind ==0:
       y=np.zeros(n)
       for m in range(1,17):
          amp=1.0/(float(m)**1.3)
          phi=rng.uniform(0.0,2.0*np.pi)
          y += amp*np.cos(0.5*np.pi*m*x + phi)
       y -= y.mean()
       mr = np.max(y)-np.min(y)
       if mr > 1e-12: y *= 0.95/mr
       h = 0.5 + y
    elif kind ==1:
       b=rng.rand(n) < 0.5
       # adjust sum by random permutation? project handles
       m_rad=int(max(2,n//40))
       k=np.arange(-m_rad,m_rad+1)
       kern=np.exp(-0.5*(k/m_rad)**2)
       kern /= kern.sum()
       h = np.convolve(b.astype(float), kern, mode='same')
       h -= h.mean()
       mr=np.max(h)-np.min(h)
       if mr>1e-12: h*=0.95/mr
       h=0.5+h
    elif kind ==2:
       # random runs: choose number transitions ~60
       ntrans=int(max(4, min(n//4, rng.randint(4, max(5,n//2)))))
       b=np.zeros(n,bool)
       curr=bool(rng.rand()<0.5)
       b[0]=curr
       remaining=ntrans
       i=0; cnt=0
       # generate run lengths via negative binomial
       while i<n-1 and remaining>0:
          length=max(1,int(rng.exponential(n/(2.0*ntrans))) )
          j=min(n, i+length)
          for p in range(i,j): b[p]=curr
          curr = not curr; remaining-=1; i=j
       for p in range(i,n): b[p]=curr
       m_rad=int(max(2,n//50)); ... smooth, scale
    else:
       # periodic sine mix
       y=0.0
       y += 0.7*np.cos(np.pi*x + rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi))
       y += 0.3*np.cos(2*np.pi*x + rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi))
       ...
    h=np.clip(h,0.1,0.9)? Actually allow 0,1. clip 0,1; project.
    return project_feasible(h,n)
```

Let's think about exact objective vs FFT in L-BFGS with sum penalty: Since LSE objective not exact, L-BFGS can converge to approximate min of FFT objective. Exact and FFT should match to 1e-15 if no rounding? FFT convolution of length L is exact up to floating; error ~1e-14 maybe. Good.

Potential bug in FFT convolution due to using `rfft` of product: Convolution theorem: `ifft(fft(a)*fft(b))` gives circular convolution length L. Since zero pad L≥n+n-1, circular = linear. `irfft` returns real. Good.

Detailed code:

```
import numpy as np
import time
import math
from scipy.optimize import minimize, linprog

_DEADLINE = [0.0]

def _time_callback(x):
    return time.time() >= _DEADLINE[0]
```

`fft_len`:
```
def fft_len(n):
    m=2*n-1
    L=1
    while L < m:
        L <<= 1
    return L
```

`c_vec_fft`: careful with `np.flip` maybe for n=0? no.
```
def c_vec_fft(h,n):
    L=fft_len(n)
    hf=np.fft.rfft(h,L)
    vf=np.fft.rfft(1.0 - np.flip(h), L)
    conv=np.fft.irfft(hf*vf, L)
    conv=conv[:2*n-1]
    return conv[::-1] * (2.0/float(n))
```

`grad_from_weights` optimize:
```
def grad_from_weights(x, w, n):
    g=np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    dx=2.0/float(n)
    w0=w[n-1]
    if w0 != 0.0:
       g += w0*(1.0 - 2.0*x)
    # We can store base pointers? Use local var
    for d in range(1,n):
       wp=w[n-1+d]
       wn=w[n-1-d]
       s=wp+wn
       if s <= 1e-18:
           continue
       diff=wp-wn
       m=n-d
       # if diff nonzero
       g[:m] += diff
       g[d:] -= diff
       g[:m] -= s*x[d:n]
       g[d:] -= s*x[0:m]
    return g*dx
```
Check signs for g[d:] += -diff - s*x[:m] -> subtract diff and subtract s*x. Yes.

But if `s` is tiny but diff maybe same? skip. If s=1e-15, contribution tiny. okay.

`lse_fg`:
```
def lse_fg(x, n, alpha, mu):
    c=c_vec_fft(x,n)
    m=float(np.max(c))
    z=alpha*(c-m)
    zm=float(np.max(z))
    e=np.exp(z-zm)
    s=float(e.sum())
    if s <= 0.0: # fallback
       w=np.zeros(len(c)); w[n-1]=1.0; val=m
    else:
       val = m + (zm + float(np.log(s)))/alpha
       w=e/s
    if mu != 0.0:
       diff=float(x.sum()) - 0.5*float(n)
       val += mu*diff*diff
    g=grad_from_weights(x,w,n)
    if mu != 0.0:
       diff=float(x.sum()) - 0.5*float(n)
       g = g + (2.0*mu*diff)*np.ones(n) # but creates array; okay
    return float(val), g
```
If s zero impossible; but if so, w all zeros -> grad zero, bad. Not.
Penalty gradient addition: `g.fill?` We can do `g += 2.0*mu*diff` works broadcasts scalar to array. That's fine. Use `g += 2.0*mu*diff`.

`shift_grad_single`:
```
def shift_grad_single(h,n,idx):
    dx=2.0/float(n)
    g=np.zeros(n)
    k=idx-(n-1)
    if k>=0:
       nv=n-k
       if nv>0:
          g[0:nv] += (1.0 - h[k:n])*dx
          g[k:n] += (-h[0:nv])*dx
    else:
       kk=-k; nv=n-kk
       if nv>0:
          g[kk:n] += (1.0 - h[0:nv])*dx
          g[0:nv] += (-h[kk:n])*dx
    return g
```
`c5_exact` as above.

`project_feasible` exact:
```
def project_feasible(h,n):
    h=np.array(h, dtype=float, copy=True)
    h=np.clip(h,0.0,1.0)
    target=0.5*float(n)
    for _ in range(30):
        s=float(h.sum())
        diff=target-s
        if abs(diff)<1e-13: break
        if diff>0:
            room=1.0-h
            tot=float(room.sum())
            if tot<1e-15: break
            frac=diff/tot
            if frac > 1.0: frac=1.0
            h += frac*room
        else:
            tot=s
            if tot<1e-15:
                h.fill(target/float(n)); break
            h *= (target/tot)
            # after scaling, maybe break if close
            if abs((target - float(h.sum()))) < 1e-13: break
    # exact adjust
    diff=target - float(h.sum())
    if abs(diff) > 1e-12:
       if diff > 0:
          order=np.argsort(h)
          rem=diff
          for i in order:
             room=1.0-h[i]
             if room > 0.0:
                inc=rem if rem < room else room
                h[i]+=inc; rem-=inc
                if abs(rem) <= 1e-14: break
       else:
          order=np.argsort(-h)
          rem=-diff
          for i in order:
             if h[i] > 0.0:
                dec=rem if rem < h[i] else h[i]
                h[i]-=dec; rem-=dec
                if abs(rem) <= 1e-14: break
    # if still not exact due weird, adjust last
    diff=target - float(h.sum())
    if abs(diff) > 1e-14:
       i=int(np.argmin(np.abs(np.concatenate((h, 1.0-h)))))? not needed.
    return np.clip(h,0.0,1.0)
```
If after adjust rem not zero due no room? target within [0,n], there is room. Fine.
Scaling for diff<0 may make some small negatives? no.

`resample_h` as above.

```
def random_smooth_candidate(n, seed, kind=0):
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed % 2147483563)
    x=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n)
    h=np.full(n,0.5)
    if kind == 0:
       y=np.zeros(n)
       for m in range(1,17):
          amp=1.0/(float(m)**1.25)
          phi=rng.uniform(0.0,2.0*np.pi)
          y += amp*np.cos(0.5*np.pi*float(m)*x + phi)
       y -= y.mean()
       span=np.max(y)-np.min(y)
       if span>1e-15: y *= 0.95/span
       h=0.5+y
    elif kind == 1:
       b=rng.rand(n) < 0.5
       rad=max(2,int(n//40))
       k=np.arange(-rad, rad+1)
       kern=np.exp(-0.5*(k/float(rad))**2)
       kern /= kern.sum()
       h=np.convolve(b.astype(float), kern, mode='same')
       y=h-h.mean(); span=...; h=0.5+y
    elif kind == 2:
       ntrans=int(max(4, min(n//2, rng.randint(6, max(7, n//3)))))
       b=np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
       cur=rng.rand()<0.5
       i=0
       rem=ntrans
       while i<n and rem>0:
          avg=max(1.0, float(n)/(2.0*ntrans))
          length=int(rng.exponential(avg))
          if length<1: length=1
          j=min(n, i+length)
          b[i:j]=1.0 if cur else 0.0
          cur = not cur
          rem -=1
          i=j
       b[i:]=1.0 if cur else 0.0
       rad=max(2,int(n//50)); smooth
       y=h-h.mean(); span; h=0.5+y
    else:
       y=0.6*np.cos(np.pi*x + rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)) + 0.35*np.cos(2*np.pi*x + rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)) + 0.2*np.cos(3*np.pi*x + rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi))
       y-=y.mean(); scale; h=0.5+y
    h=np.clip(h,0.0,1.0)
    return project_feasible(h,n)
```

`run_lbfgs` with bounds creation:
```
def run_lbfgs(h,n,phase_deadline,alphas=None,mu=0.1,maxiter=300):
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=c5_exact(h,n)
    if alphas is None: alphas=(300,1500,6000)
    bounds=[(0.0,1.0) for _ in range(n)]
    for alpha in alphas:
       if time.time() >= phase_deadline - 2: break
       _DEADLINE[0] = phase_deadline
       try:
          res=minimize(lse_fg, h, args=(n, alpha, mu), jac=True, method='L-BFGS-B',
                      bounds=bounds, callback=_time_callback,
                      options={'maxiter': maxiter, 'ftol':1e-16, 'gtol':1e-12,
                               'maxfun': max(2000, 4*maxiter), 'maxls':30})
       except Exception:
          break
       if not hasattr(res,'x'): continue
       h_new=project_feasible(res.x,n)
       c_new=c5_exact(h_new,n)
       if c_new < best_c - 1e-14:
          best_h=h_new; best_c=c_new; h=h_new
       elif c_new <= best_c + 1e-12:
          h=h_new
       else:
          h=best_h.copy()
    return project_feasible(best_h,n), c5_exact(project_feasible(best_h,n), n)
```
Maybe final project recompute c. Good.
Note: `mu=0.1` may alter objective; final exact no penalty. Fine.

```

`try_delta`:
```
def try_delta(h, delta, n, phase_deadline):
    h=project_feasible(h,n) # ensure
    best_c=c5_exact(h,n); best_h=h
    alphas=(1.0,0.6,0.4,0.2,0.1,0.05,0.02)
    for a in alphas:
       if time.time() >= phase_deadline: break
       h_new=h + a*delta
       if h_new.min() < -1e-10 or h_new.max() > 1+1e-10 or abs(float(h_new.sum())-0.5*n)>1e-10:
          h_new=project_feasible(h_new,n)
       c_new=c5_exact(h_new,n)
       if c_new < best_c - 1e-14:
          best_c=c_new; best_h=h_new
    return project_feasible(best_h,n), c5_exact(best_h,n)
```
Since `project_feasible` at end recomputes. Good.

`gradient_direction` for fallback:
```
def gradient_direction_step(h,n,phase_deadline,rho,alpha=3000):
   val,g=lse_fg(h,n,alpha,0.0)
   d=-g
   d -= d.mean()
   norm=float(np.linalg.norm(d))
   if norm < 1e-12: return h,c5_exact(h,n)
   d *= rho/norm
   return try_delta(h,d,n,phase_deadline)
```
Use in lp_polish after no_improve>3.

`lp_polish`:
```
def lp_polish(h,n,phase_deadline,rho=0.05,max_iter=300,max_active=40,tol_frac=0.004):
    h=project_feasible(h,n); best_c=c5_exact(h,n); best_h=h
    no_improve=0
    for it in range(max_iter):
       if time.time() >= phase_deadline: break
       cvec=c_vec_fft(h,n)
       active=active_indices(cvec,max_active,tol_frac)
       delta,t=lp_step(h,n,active,rho)
       if delta is not None and float(np.max(np.abs(delta))) > 1e-13:
           h_try,c_try=try_delta(h,delta,n,phase_deadline)
           if c_try < best_c - 1e-14:
               best_h=h_try; best_c=c_try; h=h_try; no_improve=0
               if rho < 0.12: rho = min(0.12, rho*1.2)
           else:
               no_improve +=1
               rho *= 0.8
       else:
           no_improve +=1
           rho *= 0.8
       if no_improve > 4:
           h_try,c_try=gradient_direction_step(h,n,phase_deadline,min(rho,0.05),2000)
           if c_try < best_c - 1e-14:
               best_h=h_try; best_c=c_try; h=h_try; no_improve=0
           else:
               no_improve +=1
           if no_improve > 12:
               break
       if rho < 1e-6:
           break
    return project_feasible(best_h,n), c5_exact(best_h,n)
```
If gradient step no improve, no_improve increment potentially >12 break. Good.

SLSQP functions:
```
def slqp_obj(x,n,alpha): val,g=lse_fg(x,n,alpha,0.0); return val
def slqp_jac(x,n,alpha): val,g=lse_fg(x,n,alpha,0.0); return g
def sum_eq(x,n): return float(x.sum()-0.5*n)
def sum_eq_jac(x,n): return np.ones(n)
def slqp_polish(h,n,phase_deadline,alpha=20000,maxiter=80):
    h=project_feasible(h,n); best_c=c5_exact(h,n)
    if n > 600: return h,best_c
    bounds=[(0.0,1.0) for _ in range(n)]
    cons=[{'type':'eq','fun':sum_eq,'jac':sum_eq_jac,'args':(n,)}]
    try:
       res=minimize(slqp_obj, h, args=(n,alpha), jac=slqp_jac, method='SLSQP', bounds=bounds, constraints=cons, options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-14})
    except Exception: return ...
    if hasattr(res,'x'):
       h_new=project_feasible(res.x,n); c_new=c5_exact(h_new,n)
       if c_new < best_c: return h_new,c_new
    return h,best_c
```
SLSQP no time callback; maxiter small. Could use `phase_deadline` only check before.

def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
   t0=time.time()
   total=min(float(budget_s), 1100.0) maybe if budget >1100? The task says 1100. Respect passed maybe. `horizon=t0+max(20.0,total)-5`
   _DEADLINE[0]=horizon
   # initial
   best_h=None; best_c=1e9; best_n=0
   init=None
   try: init=np.array(initial_h_values,float).copy()
   except: init=None
   if init is not None and len(init)>=4:
       n0=len(init); h0=project_feasible(init,n0); c0=c5_exact(h0,n0); best_h=h0; best_n=n0; best_c=c0
   else:
       n0=512; h0=random_smooth_candidate(n0,seed,0); c0=...; best...
   # Low-res
   remaining = horizon-time.time()
   if remaining > 250:
       n_low=256
       low_end = time.time() + min(120.0, remaining*0.15)
       low_end = min(low_end, horizon-350.0)
       cands=[]
       if init is not None: cands.append(resample_h(init,n_low))
       cands.append(random_smooth_candidate(n_low, seed+1,0))
       cands.append(random_smooth_candidate(n_low, seed+2,1))
       cands.append(random_smooth_candidate(n_low, seed+3,2))
       cands.append(random_smooth_candidate(n_low, seed+4,3))
       # maybe remove cands worse than best_c+0.05? no, optimize may improve.
       per = max(5.0, (low_end-time.time()) / max(1,len(cands)))
       for hc in cands:
           if time.time() >= low_end - 2: break
           phase = time.time() + per
           phase = min(phase, low_end, horizon-250)
           if phase - time.time() < 3: continue
           hc2,c = run_lbfgs(hc, n_low, phase, alphas=(300,1500,5000), mu=0.1, maxiter=100)
           if c < best_c:
              best_h=hc2; best_n=n_low; best_c=c
   # Main high
   remaining=horizon-time.time()
   if remaining > 350:
      n_main=1024 if remaining > 700 else 512
      main_candidates=[]
      if init is not None: main_candidates.append(resample_h(init,n_main))
      if best_h is not None: main_candidates.append(resample_h(best_h,n_main))
      # maybe a random high if remaining >900
      if remaining > 900: main_candidates.append(random_smooth_candidate(n_main, seed+20,1))
      # choose best candidate exact
      h_main=None; c_main=1e9
      for hc in main_candidates:
          he,ce=safe_evaluate(hc,n_main)
          if ce < c_main: c_main=ce; h_main=he
      if h_main is not None:
          # L-BFGS phase1
          rem=horizon-time.time()
          t1=time.time()+min(300.0, rem*0.4, rem-300)
          if t1 > time.time()+10 and t1 < horizon:
              h_main,c_main = run_lbfgs(h_main,n_main,t1, alphas=(300,1500,6000), mu=0.1, maxiter=400)
              if c_main < best_c: update
          # LP
          rem=horizon-time.time()
          t2=time.time()+min(220.0, rem*0.3, rem-250)
          if t2 > time.time()+5:
              h_main,c_main=lp_polish(h_main,n_main,t2, rho=0.06, max_iter=300, max_active=40, tol_frac=0.004)
              update
          # L-BFGS 2
          rem=horizon-time.time()
          t3=time.time()+min(120.0, rem*0.2, rem-200)
          if t3 > time.time()+5:
              h_main,c_main=run_lbfgs(h_main,n_main,t3, alphas=(6000,15000), mu=0.05, maxiter=300)
              update
          # LP 2
          rem=horizon-time.time()
          t4=time.time()+min(80.0, rem*0.15, rem-150)
          if t4 > time.time()+3:
              h_main,c_main=lp_polish(h_main,n_main,t4, rho=0.03, max_iter=150, max_active=24, tol_frac=0.002)
              update
   # High-res final
   rem=horizon-time.time()
   if rem > 400:
      if best_n == 0 or best_n < 2048 or True: # we can upsample global best to 2048/>
          # Use the candidate that currently has lowest c among high? We have best_h. If best_n lower, upsample; if best_n high maybe already.
          n_fin = 2048 if rem > 700 else 1536
          if n_fin > 0 and n_fin != best_n:
              h_fin=resample_h(best_h,n_fin)
          else: h_fin=project_feasible(best_h,best_n)
          # L-BFGS
          t5=time.time()+min(180.0, rem*0.45, rem-120)
          if t5 > time.time()+5:
             h_fin,c_fin=run_lbfgs(h_fin,n_fin,t5, alphas=(2000,8000), mu=0.05, maxiter=300)
             if c_fin < best_c: update best_h,best_n
          # LP
          rem=horizon-time.time()
          t6=time.time()+min(80.0, rem*0.3, rem-60)
          if t6 > time.time()+3:
             h_fin,c_fin=lp_polish(h_fin,n_fin,t6, rho=0.03, max_iter=120, max_active=24, tol_frac=0.002)
             update
   # Final polish global best
   rem=horizon-time.time()
   if rem > 60:
      t7=time.time()+min(40.0, rem-10)
      if best_n <= 600:
          h_final,c_final=slqp_polish(best_h,best_n,t7, alpha=30000, maxiter=50)
      else:
          h_final,c_final=lp_polish(best_h,best_n,t7, rho=0.02, max_iter=80, max_active=20, tol_frac=0.001)
      if c_final < best_c: update
   if rem > 30:
      # final L-BFGS high alpha small time? maybe
      h_final,c_final=run_lbfgs(best_h,best_n,time.time()+min(20,rem-5), alphas=(20000,), mu=0.02, maxiter=100)
      update
   best_h=project_feasible(best_h,best_n); best_c=c5_exact(best_h,best_n)
   return best_h,best_c,best_n
```

Thus we must fix all shift-specific gradient formulas to match np.correlate.

Let's derive correct mapping:
For full correlate h, 1-h, index m.
Let d = abs(m - (n-1)).
If m < n-1: index corresponds to negative spatial shift? Actual f = ∑_{i=d}^{n-1} h_i (1 - h_{i-d})? For m=n-1-d: examples n=3,d=1: a(1-b)+b(1-c). This is pairs (i, i+d) with first lower. So f = ∑_{i=0}^{n-1-d} h_i (1 - h_{i+d}). Let's call "right product" (left times complement right). For m=n-1+d: f=∑_{i=0}^{n-1-d} h_{i+d} (1 - h_i) = ∑_{j=d}^{n-1} h_j (1 - h_{j-d}). "left product".
Thus for pair (i,j=i+d), there are two c entries:
- left index L = n-1-d: pair term h_i (1 - h_j)
- right index R = n-1+d: pair term h_j (1 - h_i)
Therefore, if we name weights by index: w_L for lower-left term, w_R for right-left term.
Gradient for pair:
From L: grad_i += w_L(1-h_j), grad_j += w_L(-h_i)
From R: grad_i += w_R(-h_j), grad_j += w_R(1-h_i)
Total:
g_i += (w_L - w_R) - (w_L + w_R) h_j
g_j += (w_R - w_L) - (w_L + w_R) h_i
Let `A = w_L + w_R`, `diff = w_L - w_R` (where L = index n-1-d, R=n-1+d). Then g_lower += diff - A*h_upper; g_upper += -diff - A*h_lower.
Previously we used wp=w[n-1+d], wn=w[n-1-d], diff=wp-wn. That is diff_RL negative of correct. Need swap:
`w_lower = w[n-1-d]` (term h_i(1-h_j)), `w_upper = w[n-1+d]` (term h_j(1-h_i)).
`diff = w_lower - w_upper`.
Then same formula with s=sum. Good.
Diagonal index n-1 weight.

```
wl=w[n-1-d]; wr=w[n-1+d]; s=wl+wr; diff=wl-wr
g[:m] += diff; g[d:] -= diff; g[:m] -= s*x[d:]; g[d:] -= s*x[:m]
```

Now `shift_grad_single` must be fixed:
- If idx < n-1: d = (n-1)-idx >0. This is L term f=∑_{i=0}^{n-1-d} h_i (1 - h_{i+d}). Gradient:
  g[0:nv] = (1 - h[d:n])
  g[d:n] += -h[0:nv]
- If idx > n-1: d=idx-(n-1) >0. This is R term f=∑_{i=0}^{n-1-d} h_{i+d} (1 - h_i). Gradient:
  g[0:nv] += -h[d:n] (derivative wrt lower)
  g[d:n] += (1 - h[0:nv])
- If idx==n-1: f=∑h_i(1-h_i); g=1-2h.
So corrected:
```
def shift_grad_single(h,n,idx):
 dx=...
 g=zeros
 d=abs(idx-(n-1))
 if d==0: g=(1-2h)*dx
 else:
   nv=n-d
   if idx < n-1: # left term lower*(1-upper)
      g[0:nv] = (1-h[d:n])*dx
      g[d:n] += (-h[0:nv])*dx
   else:
      g[0:nv] = (-h[d:n])*dx
      g[d:n] += (1-h[0:nv])*dx
 return g
```

```
def grad_from_weights(x,w,n):
   dx=2.0/float(n)
   nz = int(np.count_nonzero(w > 1e-15))
   if n <= 300 or nz < max(16, n//4):
       g=np.zeros(n)
       w0=w[n-1]
       if w0: g += w0*(1-2*x)
       for d in range(1,n):
           wl=w[n-1-d]; wr=w[n-1+d]; s=wl+wr
           if s <= 1e-18: continue
           diff=wl-wr
           m=n-d
           g[:m] += diff
           g[d:] -= diff
           g[:m] -= s*x[d:n]
           g[d:] -= s*x[:m]
       return g*dx
   # fft
   A=np.zeros(n); B=np.zeros(n)
   # Use vectorized? indices
   d=np.arange(1,n)
   wl=w[n-1-d]; wr=w[n-1+d]
   A[1:]=wl-wr
   B[1:]=wl+wr
   K=np.zeros(2*n-1)
   K[n-1-d]=B[1:]
   K[n-1+d]=B[1:]
   L=1
   mneed=n + (2*n-1) -1
   while L<mneed: L <<=1
   hx=np.fft.rfft(x,L)
   Kf=np.fft.rfft(K,L)
   conv=np.fft.irfft(hx*Kf,L)
   S=conv[n-1:n-1+n]
   diff=np.zeros(n+1)
   # vectorize difference? We can loop d but use cumsum. A[1:].
   # For each d, diff[0]+=A[d], diff[n-d]-=A[d], diff[d]-=A[d], diff[n]+=A[d]
   # We can use numpy advanced assignment with accumulate:
   a=A[1:]
   idx_pos=np.arange(1,n) # d
   diff[0] = np.sum(a)
   # diff[n-d] for d=1..n-1 -> indices n-1..1 descending. Use np.add.at
   np.add.at(diff, n - idx_pos, -a)
   np.add.at(diff, idx_pos, -a)
   diff[n] = np.sum(a)
   T=np.cumsum(diff[:n])
   g = w0*(1.0-2.0*x) + T - S
   return g*dx
```

```python
import numpy as np
import time
import math
from scipy.optimize import minimize, linprog

_DEADLINE = [0.0]


def _time_callback(x):
    return time.time() >= _DEADLINE[0]


def phase_time(now, horizon, fraction, reserve):
    rem = horizon - now
    if rem <= 0.0:
        return now
    return min(horizon, now + min(fraction * rem, reserve))


def fft_len(n, extra=1):
    m = n * extra
    L = 1
    while L < m:
        L <<= 1
    return L


def c_vec_exact(h, n):
    return np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full") * (2.0 / float(n))


def c5_exact(h, n):
    c = c_vec_exact(h, n)
    return float(np.max(c))


def c_vec_fft(h, n):
    L = fft_len(n, 2)
    hf = np.fft.rfft(h, L)
    vf = np.fft.rfft(1.0 - np.flip(h), L)
    conv = np.fft.irfft(hf * vf, L)
    return conv[:2 * n - 1][::-1] * (2.0 / float(n))


def grad_from_weights_direct(x, w, n):
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    g = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    w0 = w[n - 1]
    if w0 != 0.0:
        g += w0 * (1.0 - 2.0 * x)
    for d in range(1, n):
        wl = w[n - 1 - d]
        wr = w[n - 1 + d]
        s = wl + wr
        if s <= 1e-18:
            continue
        diff = wl - wr
        m = n - d
        g[:m] += diff
        g[d:] -= diff
        g[:m] -= s * x[d:n]
        g[d:] -= s * x[:m]
    return g * dx


def grad_from_weights(x, w, n):
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    nz = int(np.count_nonzero(w > 1e-15))
    if n <= 300 or nz < max(16, n // 4):
        return grad_from_weights_direct(x, w, n)

    w0 = w[n - 1]

    d = np.arange(1, n)
    wl = w[n - 1 - d]
    wr = w[n - 1 + d]
    a = wl - wr
    b = wl + wr

    K = np.zeros(2 * n - 1, dtype=float)
    if n > 1:
        K[:n - 1] = b[::-1]
        K[n:] = b

    need = n + (2 * n - 1) - 1
    L = 1
    while L < need:
        L <<= 1
    hx = np.fft.rfft(x, L)
    kf = np.fft.rfft(K, L)
    conv = np.fft.irfft(hx * kf, L)
    S = conv[n - 1:n - 1 + n]

    diff = np.zeros(n + 1, dtype=float)
    for i in range(1, n):
        av = a[i - 1]
        if av == 0.0:
            continue
        diff[0] += av
        diff[n - i] -= av
        diff[i] -= av
        diff[n] += av
    T = np.cumsum(diff[:n])

    g = w0 * (1.0 - 2.0 * x) + T - S
    return g * dx


def lse_fg(x, n, alpha, mu):
    c = c_vec_fft(x, n)
    m = float(np.max(c))
    z = alpha * (c - m)
    zm = float(np.max(z))
    e = np.exp(z - zm)
    s = float(e.sum())

    if s <= 0.0:
        w = np.zeros(len(c), dtype=float)
        w[n - 1] = 1.0
        val = m
    else:
        val = m + (zm + float(np.log(s))) / alpha
        w = e / s

    if mu != 0.0:
        dsum = float(np.sum(x)) - 0.5 * float(n)
        val += mu * dsum * dsum

    g = grad_from_weights(x, w, n)
    if mu != 0.0:
        dsum = float(np.sum(x)) - 0.5 * float(n)
        g += 2.0 * mu * dsum

    return float(val), g


def shift_grad_single(h, n, idx):
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    g = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    center = n - 1
    d = abs(idx - center)
    if d == 0:
        g += (1.0 - 2.0 * h) * dx
        return g

    nv = n - d
    if idx < center:
        # term: sum_i h[i] * (1 - h[i+d])
        g[:nv] = (1.0 - h[d:n]) * dx
        g[d:n] += (-h[:nv]) * dx
    else:
        # term: sum_i h[i+d] * (1 - h[i])
        g[:nv] = (-h[d:n]) * dx
        g[d:n] += (1.0 - h[:nv]) * dx
    return g


def project_feasible(h, n):
    h = np.array(h, dtype=float, copy=True)
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    target = 0.5 * float(n)

    for _ in range(30):
        s = float(h.sum())
        diff = target - s
        if abs(diff) < 1e-13:
            break
        if diff > 0.0:
            room = 1.0 - h
            tot = float(room.sum())
            if tot < 1e-15:
                break
            frac = diff / tot
            if frac > 1.0:
                frac = 1.0
            h += frac * room
        else:
            tot = s
            if tot < 1e-15:
                h.fill(target / float(n))
                break
            h *= (target / tot)
            if abs(target - float(h.sum())) < 1e-13:
                break

    diff = target - float(h.sum())
    if abs(diff) > 1e-12:
        if diff > 0.0:
            order = np.argsort(h)
            rem = diff
            for i in order:
                room = 1.0 - h[i]
                if room > 0.0:
                    inc = rem if rem < room else room
                    h[i] += inc
                    rem -= inc
                    if abs(rem) <= 1e-14:
                        break
        else:
            order = np.argsort(-h)
            rem = -diff
            for i in order:
                if h[i] > 0.0:
                    dec = rem if rem < h[i] else h[i]
                    h[i] -= dec
                    rem -= dec
                    if abs(rem) <= 1e-14:
                        break

    diff = target - float(h.sum())
    if abs(diff) > 1e-14:
        if diff > 0.0:
            idx = int(np.argmin(h))
            inc = min(diff, max(0.0, 1.0 - h[idx]))
            h[idx] += inc
        else:
            idx = int(np.argmax(h))
            dec = min(-diff, max(0.0, h[idx]))
            h[idx] -= dec

    return np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)


def resample_h(h, n_new):
    n_old = len(h)
    if n_old == n_new:
        return project_feasible(h, n_new)
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n_old)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n_new)
    h_new = np.interp(x_new, x_old, h)
    return project_feasible(h_new, n_new)


def safe_evaluate(h, n):
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    c = c5_exact(h, n)
    return h, c


def random_smooth_candidate(n, seed, kind=0):
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed % 2147483563)
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n)
    h = np.full(n, 0.5, dtype=float)

    if kind == 0:
        y = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
        for m in range(1, 17):
            amp = 1.0 / (float(m) ** 1.25)
            phi = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi)
            y += amp * np.cos(0.5 * np.pi * float(m) * x + phi)
        y -= y.mean()
        span = float(np.max(y) - np.min(y))
        if span > 1e-15:
            y *= 0.95 / span
        h = 0.5 + y

    elif kind == 1:
        b = (rng.rand(n) < 0.5).astype(float)
        rad = max(2, int(n // 40))
        k = np.arange(-rad, rad + 1)
        kern = np.exp(-0.5 * (k / float(rad)) ** 2)
        kern /= kern.sum()
        h = np.convolve(b, kern, mode="same")
        y = h - h.mean()
        span = float(np.max(y) - np.min(y))
        if span > 1e-15:
            y *= 0.95 / span
        h = 0.5 + y

    elif kind == 2:
        low = 6
        high = max(7, n // 3)
        ntrans = int(rng.randint(low, high))
        b = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
        cur = bool(rng.rand() < 0.5)
        i = 0
        rem = ntrans
        while i < n and rem > 0:
            avg = max(1.0, float(n) / (2.0 * float(max(1, ntrans))))
            length = int(rng.exponential(avg))
            if length < 1:
                length = 1
            j = min(n, i + length)
            b[i:j] = 1.0 if cur else 0.0
            cur = not cur
            rem -= 1
            i = j
        b[i:] = 1.0 if cur else 0.0

        rad = max(2, int(n // 50))
        k = np.arange(-rad, rad + 1)
        kern = np.exp(-0.5 * (k / float(rad)) ** 2)
        kern /= kern.sum()
        h = np.convolve(b, kern, mode="same")
        y = h - h.mean()
        span = float(np.max(y) - np.min(y))
        if span > 1e-15:
            y *= 0.95 / span
        h = 0.5 + y

    else:
        y = (
            0.62 * np.cos(np.pi * x + rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
            + 0.34 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * x + rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
            + 0.18 * np.cos(3.0 * np.pi * x + rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        )
        y -= y.mean()
        span = float(np.max(y) - np.min(y))
        if span > 1e-15:
            y *= 0.95 / span
        h = 0.5 + y

    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    return project_feasible(h, n)


def run_lbfgs(h, n, phase_deadline, alphas=None, mu=1.0, maxiter=300):
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = c5_exact(h, n)

    if alphas is None:
        alphas = (300.0, 1500.0, 6000.0)

    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0) for _ in range(n)]

    for alpha in alphas:
        if time.time() >= phase_deadline - 2.0:
            break
        _DEADLINE[0] = phase_deadline
        try:
            res = minimize(
                lse_fg,
                h,
                args=(n, alpha, mu),
                jac=True,
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                bounds=bounds,
                callback=_time_callback,
                options={
                    "maxiter": maxiter,
                    "ftol": 1e-16,
                    "gtol": 1e-12,
                    "maxfun": max(2000, 4 * maxiter),
                    "maxls": 30,
                },
            )
        except Exception:
            break

        if not hasattr(res, "x"):
            continue

        h_new = project_feasible(res.x, n)
        c_new = c5_exact(h_new, n)

        if c_new < best_c - 1e-14:
            best_h = h_new
            best_c = c_new
            h = h_new
        elif c_new <= best_c + 1e-12:
            h = h_new
        else:
            h = best_h.copy()

    h = project_feasible(best_h, n)
    return h, c5_exact(h, n)


def active_indices(cvec, max_active=40, tol_frac=0.004):
    cm = float(np.max(cvec))
    tol = max(1e-10, tol_frac * cm)
    idx = np.where(cvec >= cm - tol)[0]
    if idx.size == 0:
        idx = np.array([int(np.argmax(cvec))], dtype=int)
    elif idx.size > max_active:
        order = np.argsort(-cvec[idx])[:max_active]
        idx = idx[order]
    return idx


def lp_step(h, n, active, rho):
    nv = n + 1
    c_obj = np.zeros(nv, dtype=float)
    c_obj[-1] = 1.0

    cvec = c_vec_fft(h, n)
    m = len(active)
    A = np.zeros((m, nv), dtype=float)
    b = np.zeros(m, dtype=float)

    for j, idx in enumerate(active):
        g = shift_grad_single(h, n, int(idx))
        A[j, :n] = g
        A[j, n] = -1.0
        b[j] = -cvec[int(idx)]

    A_eq = np.ones((1, nv), dtype=float)
    A_eq[0, n] = 0.0
    b_eq = np.zeros(1, dtype=float)

    lo = -np.minimum(rho, h)
    hi = np.minimum(rho, 1.0 - h)
    bounds = [(float(lo[i]), float(hi[i])) for i in range(n)]
    bounds.append((0.0, 1.0))

    try:
        res = linprog(
            c_obj,
            A_ub=A,
            b_ub=b,
            A_eq=A_eq,
            b_eq=b_eq,
            bounds=bounds,
            method="highs",
        )
        if res.success:
            return res.x[:n].copy(), float(res.x[n])
    except Exception:
        pass
    return None, None


def try_delta(h, delta, n, phase_deadline):
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = c5_exact(h, n)

    alphas = (1.0, 0.5, 0.2, 0.05)
    for a in alphas:
        if time.time() >= phase_deadline:
            break
        h_new = h + a * delta
        if h_new.min() < -1e-10 or h_new.max() > 1.0 + 1e-10:
            h_new = np.clip(h_new, 0.0, 1.0)
        if abs(float(h_new.sum()) - 0.5 * float(n)) > 1e-10:
            h_new = project_feasible(h_new, n)
        c_new = c5_exact(h_new, n)
        if c_new < best_c - 1e-14:
            best_c = c_new
            best_h = h_new

    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, n)
    return best_h, c5_exact(best_h, n)


def gradient_direction_step(h, n, phase_deadline, rho, alpha=3000.0):
    _, g = lse_fg(h, n, alpha, 0.0)
    d = -g
    d -= d.mean()
    norm = float(np.linalg.norm(d))
    if norm < 1e-12:
        h = project_feasible(h, n)
        return h, c5_exact(h, n)
    d *= rho / norm
    return try_delta(h, d, n, phase_deadline)


def lp_polish(h, n, phase_deadline, rho=0.05, max_iter=300, max_active=40, tol_frac=0.004):
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = c5_exact(h, n)
    no_improve = 0

    for _ in range(max_iter):
        if time.time() >= phase_deadline:
            break

        cvec = c_vec_fft(h, n)
        active = active_indices(cvec, max_active=max_active, tol_frac=tol_frac)
        delta, _ = lp_step(h, n, active, rho)

        progressed = False
        if delta is not None and float(np.max(np.abs(delta))) > 1e-13:
            h_try, c_try = try_delta(h, delta, n, phase_deadline)
            if c_try < best_c - 1e-14:
                best_h = h_try
                best_c = c_try
                h = h_try
                no_improve = 0
                progressed = True
                if rho < 0.12:
                    rho = min(0.12, rho * 1.2)

        if not progressed:
            no_improve += 1
            rho *= 0.8

        if no_improve > 3:
            h_try, c_try = gradient_direction_step(
                h, n, phase_deadline, min(rho, 0.04), alpha=2500.0
            )
            if c_try < best_c - 1e-14:
                best_h = h_try
                best_c = c_try
                h = h_try
                no_improve = 0
            else:
                no_improve += 1
                if no_improve > 14:
                    break

        if rho < 1e-6:
            break

    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, n)
    return best_h, c5_exact(best_h, n)


def slqp_obj(x, n, alpha):
    val, _ = lse_fg(x, n, alpha, 0.0)
    return val


def slqp_jac(x, n, alpha):
    _, g = lse_fg(x, n, alpha, 0.0)
    return g


def sum_eq_fun(x, n):
    return float(np.sum(x) - 0.5 * float(n))


def sum_eq_jac(x, n):
    return np.ones(n, dtype=float)


def slqp_polish(h, n, phase_deadline, alpha=25000.0, maxiter=40):
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    best_c = c5_exact(h, n)
    if n > 320:
        return h, best_c

    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0) for _ in range(n)]
    cons = [{"type": "eq", "fun": sum_eq_fun, "jac": sum_eq_jac, "args": (n,)}]

    try:
        res = minimize(
            slqp_obj,
            h,
            args=(n, alpha),
            jac=slqp_jac,
            method="SLSQP",
            bounds=bounds,
            constraints=cons,
            options={"maxiter": maxiter, "ftol": 1e-14},
        )
    except Exception:
        return h, best_c

    if hasattr(res, "x"):
        h_new = project_feasible(res.x, n)
        c_new = c5_exact(h_new, n)
        if c_new < best_c:
            return h_new, c_new

    return h, best_c


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    try:
        total_budget = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        total_budget = 1100.0
    if not np.isfinite(total_budget) or total_budget <= 0.0:
        total_budget = 1100.0
    horizon = t0 + max(15.0, total_budget) - 5.0
    _DEADLINE[0] = horizon

    init = None
    try:
        init = np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float)
        if init.ndim != 1:
            init = init.reshape(-1)
    except Exception:
        init = None

    best_h = None
    best_n = 0
    best_c = 1e100

    if init is not None and len(init) >= 4:
        n0 = int(len(init))
        h0 = project_feasible(init, n0)
        c0 = c5_exact(h0, n0)
        best_h = h0
        best_n = n0
        best_c = c0
    else:
        n0 = 512
        h0 = random_smooth_candidate(n0, seed, 0)
        h0 = project_feasible(h0, n0)
        c0 = c5_exact(h0, n0)
        best_h = h0
        best_n = n0
        best_c = c0

    # Low-resolution escapes.
    rem = horizon - time.time()
    if rem > 500.0:
        n_low = 256
        t_low = min(horizon, time.time() + min(0.12 * rem, 150.0))
        if t_low - time.time() > 10.0:
            raw_cands = []
            if init is not None:
                raw_cands.append(resample_h(init, n_low))
            raw_cands.append(random_smooth_candidate(n_low, seed + 1, 0))
            raw_cands.append(random_smooth_candidate(n_low, seed + 2, 1))
            raw_cands.append(random_smooth_candidate(n_low, seed + 3, 2))
            raw_cands.append(random_smooth_candidate(n_low, seed + 4, 3))

            evals = []
            for hc in raw_cands:
                he, ce = safe_evaluate(hc, n_low)
                if ce < best_c + 0.05:
                    evals.append((ce, he))
            evals.sort()

            if evals:
                per = max(6.0, (t_low - time.time()) / float(len(evals)))
                for ce, he in evals:
                    if time.time() >= t_low - 5.0:
                        break
                    phase = min(t_low, time.time() + per)
                    if phase - time.time() < 3.0:
                        continue
                    he2, ce2 = run_lbfgs(
                        he,
                        n_low,
                        phase,
                        alphas=(300.0, 1500.0, 5000.0),
                        mu=1.0,
                        maxiter=120,
                    )
                    if ce2 < best_c:
                        best_h = he2
                        best_n = n_low
                        best_c = ce2

    # Main high-resolution optimization.
    rem = horizon - time.time()
    if rem > 500.0:
        n_main = 2048 if rem > 800.0 else 1536
        main_raw = []
        if init is not None:
            main_raw.append(resample_h(init, n_main))
        if best_h is not None:
            main_raw.append(resample_h(best_h, n_main))
        if rem > 900.0:
            main_raw.append(random_smooth_candidate(n_main, seed + 30, 1))

        main_evals = []
        for hc in main_raw:
            he, ce = safe_evaluate(hc, n_main)
            main_evals.append((ce, he))
        if not main_evals:
            he, ce = safe_evaluate(best_h, n_main)
            main_evals.append((ce, he))

        main_evals.sort()
        c_main, h_main = main_evals[0]

        if c_main < best_c:
            best_h = h_main
            best_n = n_main
            best_c = c_main

        t1 = phase_time(time.time(), horizon, 0.42, 350.0)
        t1 = min(t1, horizon - 450.0)
        if t1 > time.time() + 15.0:
            h_main, c_main = run_lbfgs(
                h_main,
                n_main,
                t1,
                alphas=(300.0, 1500.0, 6000.0),
                mu=1.0,
                maxiter=500,
            )
            if c_main < best_c:
                best_h = h_main
                best_n = n_main
                best_c = c_main

        t2 = phase_time(time.time(), horizon, 0.30, 260.0)
        t2 = min(t2, horizon - 300.0)
        if t2 > time.time() + 5.0:
            h_main, c_main = lp_polish(
                h_main,
                n_main,
                t2,
                rho=0.06,
                max_iter=350,
                max_active=48,
                tol_frac=0.004,
            )
            if c_main < best_c:
                best_h = h_main
                best_n = n_main
                best_c = c_main

        t3 = phase_time(time.time(), horizon, 0.18, 150.0)
        t3 = min(t3, horizon - 200.0)
        if t3 > time.time() + 5.0:
            h_main, c_main = run_lbfgs(
                h_main,
                n_main,
                t3,
                alphas=(6000.0, 15000.0),
                mu=0.5,
                maxiter=400,
            )
            if c_main < best_c:
                best_h = h_main
                best_n = n_main
                best_c = c_main

        t4 = phase_time(time.time(), horizon, 0.10, 90.0)
        t4 = min(t4, horizon - 120.0)
        if t4 > time.time() + 3.0:
            h_main, c_main = lp_polish(
                h_main,
                n_main,
                t4,
                rho=0.03,
                max_iter=200,
                max_active=24,
                tol_frac=0.002,
            )
            if c_main < best_c:
                best_h = h_main
                best_n = n_main
                best_c = c_main

    # Final focused polish on the global best.
    rem = horizon - time.time()
    if rem > 80.0:
        t5 = phase_time(time.time(), horizon, 0.25, 80.0)
        if best_n <= 320:
            hf, cf = slqp_polish(best_h, best_n, t5, alpha=30000.0, maxiter=50)
        else:
            hf, cf = lp_polish(
                best_h,
                best_n,
                t5,
                rho=0.02,
                max_iter=120,
                max_active=16,
                tol_frac=0.001,
            )
        if cf < best_c:
            best_h = hf
            best_n = best_n
            best_c = cf

    rem = horizon - time.time()
    if rem > 25.0:
        t6 = phase_time(time.time(), horizon, 0.30, 30.0)
        hf, cf = run_lbfgs(
            best_h,
            best_n,
            t6,
            alphas=(12000.0,),
            mu=0.25,
            maxiter=160,
        )
        if cf < best_c:
            best_h = hf
            best_n = len(hf)
            best_c = cf

    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, best_n)
    c_final = c5_exact(best_h, best_n)
    if c_final < best_c:
        best_c = c_final

    return best_h, float(best_c), int(best_n)
```
